- **stalled-deal-review**: Prepare a review, from the deal record and the notes the person supplies, of every open deal that has stopped moving: the stage it stalled at, what that stage's exit still lacks, whose move it is and one question that would settle it. The same review logs each deal lost in the period with the stage it ended at, the reason as the record gives it, whose call it was and what would reopen it, if anyone said. The assistant prepares; it never changes a record, sends a message, closes a deal, moves a stage or judges a salesperson. ([Fully-Powered-AI/sales-workflow-templates](https://github.com/Fully-Powered-AI/sales-workflow-templates/tree/HEAD/stalled-deal-review))

It does NOT define commit format (standard-git-commit) or tags (standard-git-tag). ([Cordona/claude-code-crucible](https://github.com/Cordona/claude-code-crucible/tree/HEAD/software-development/agents/specialists/git-operator/skills/standard-git-branch))
- **standard-git-branching**: Company Git branching and release standard (GitHub Flow with Conventional Commits). ([projectious-work/aibox](https://github.com/projectious-work/aibox/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/standard-git-branching) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **standard-git-commit**: The single rubric for a good git COMMIT — message format, atomicity, signing, and the commit-plan approval gate. Bind whenever a commit is being authored, by the git-operator or by anything committing directly. Requires procedure-git-identity (bound alongside it) for the signing identity, which this skill assumes rather than resolves. Does not cover branch naming (standard-git-branch), release tags (standard-git-tag), or PR/MR body craft (standard-git-pr). ([Cordona/claude-code-crucible](https://github.com/Cordona/claude-code-crucible/tree/HEAD/software-development/agents/specialists/git-operator/skills/standard-git-commit))
- **standard-git-pr**: The single rubric for an excellent pull-request (GitHub) or merge-request (GitLab) body/title. Bind whenever a PR/MR is opened or its body/title is edited, to write or judge that content. Does NOT define PR mechanics (procedure-gh-pr), MR mechanics (procedure-glab-mr), either account gate (procedure-github-auth / procedure-gitlab-auth), or backlog-artifact craft (that's the project-manager's standard-backlog-artifacts, not this). ([Cordona/claude-code-crucible](https://github.com/Cordona/claude-code-crucible/tree/HEAD/software-development/agents/specialists/git-operator/skills/standard-git-pr))
- **standard-git-tag**: The rubric for a good release tag and SemVer versioning, followed by the git-operator whenever a release is cut. Pair with procedure-git-identity, which resolves and confirms the signing identity before the tag is signed — this skill does not do that itself. Does NOT define commit format (standard-git-commit) or branch naming (standard-git-branch). ([Cordona/claude-code-crucible](https://github.com/Cordona/claude-code-crucible/tree/HEAD/software-development/agents/specialists/git-operator/skills/standard-git-tag))
- **standard-git-workflow**: Git conventions for branch names, Conventional Commits message format, .gitignore essentials, solo and feature-branch workflows and commit hygiene, governed by the standing rule that an agent never commits, pushes or runs history-changing git unless the owner explicitly asks. - **standard-github**: GitHub, CI and deploy rules of the cuadrilla standard for any stack — branch model, ci.yml generated from .claude/cuadrilla.json, workflow security, the Claude PR review workflow, dependabot, CODEOWNERS and the deploy model. Does not restate artifact craft/taxonomy (that's `standard-backlog-artifacts`), define per-client template/label/status content (a `standard-jira-&lt;client&gt;` overlay), or cover the `procedure-jira`/`procedure-jira-auth` CLI mechanics.
